- **agentport-mcp**: Use when connecting to AgentPort as a native MCP client — the StreamableHTTP endpoint, the `{integration}__{tool}` naming convention, and how approval-required and denied responses come back over MCP. - **agentport-overview**: Use when you need to understand how AgentPort works — the tool gateway concept, the three call outcomes (allowed / approval required / denied), and how to handle approval-required responses. Also use whenever the user requests a task that may need a third-party integration (GitHub, Slack, Stripe, PostHog, email, etc.) — AgentPort may already have it installed or available to install, and should be checked before reaching for another path. Read this before the MCP or CLI skills. ([yakkomajuri/agentport-skills](https://github.com/yakkomajuri/agentport-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agentport-overview))

([MANVENDRA-github/agentry](https://github.com/MANVENDRA-github/agentry/tree/HEAD/.codex/agents/skills/agentry-test-author))
- **agentry-test-reviewer**: Reviews existing tests for whether they actually protect behavior — asserting real outcomes over mock calls, covering edges and failure paths, not brittle or tautological. Invoke after tests are written or when a suite passes but bugs still slip through. Returns prioritized findings on test quality and coverage gaps, not a coverage percentage. ([MANVENDRA-github/agentry](https://github.com/MANVENDRA-github/agentry/tree/HEAD/.codex/agents/skills/agentry-test-reviewer))
- **agentry-test-writing**: Writes tests for code that already exists and is untested — characterize and protect current behavior so future changes are safe. Use this for adding coverage to existing code; use tdd-workflow when writing new code test-first. ([MANVENDRA-github/agentry](https://github.com/MANVENDRA-github/agentry/tree/HEAD/.codex/agents/skills/agentry-test-writing))
- **agentry-typescript-strict-mode**: TypeScript strict mode discipline — keep every strict flag on, treat escape hatches as code smells. Apply when working in any TypeScript file. Skip for exploratory spikes or in-progress JavaScript migrations where partial strictness is the temporary goal. ([MANVENDRA-github/agentry](https://github.com/MANVENDRA-github/agentry/tree/HEAD/.codex/agents/skills/agentry-typescript-strict-mode))
- **agentry-verification-loop**: Discipline of not declaring work done until it has actually been run and observed to do what was claimed. Invoke before saying a task is complete, a bug is fixed, or a feature works. Closes the gap between "the code looks right" and "the code does the right thing." Skip only for changes you cannot run (pure docs, comments). ([MANVENDRA-github/agentry](https://github.com/MANVENDRA-github/agentry/tree/HEAD/.codex/agents/skills/agentry-verification-loop))
- **agentry-yaml-config-safety**: YAML gotchas when authoring or editing `.yml`/`.yaml` files: quote ambiguous scalars to defeat the Norway problem (`no`/`off`/`yes` parse as booleans, `1.20` truncates to a float, leading-zero values become octal or strings), indent with spaces and never tabs, reject duplicate keys, and keep anchors/aliases minimal. Invoke whenever writing or modifying a YAML config, CI, or manifest file, where a value that reads correctly can parse wrong with no error. ([MANVENDRA-github/agentry](https://github.com/MANVENDRA-github/agentry/tree/HEAD/.codex/agents/skills/agentry-yaml-config-safety))
